# ok anything else?

Yes—there are a few essential next steps before you sign a 3PL. You have the market plan and outreach emails; now you need to protect quality, tax compliance, and cash.

## Before selecting a 3PL

Ask each 3PL these non-negotiable questions:


| Requirement | What CHIAREL needs |
| :-- | :-- |
| Batch traceability | The 3PL must record the exact lot/batch shipped in every customer order—not just SKU-level inventory |
| Expiry control | FEFO: first-expired, first-out inventory rotation |
| Quarantine | Separate locked status/location for damaged, returned, expired, recalled, or suspect inventory |
| Returns | No return can go back into saleable inventory without inspection and written disposition |
| Recall support | Ability to quarantine a lot quickly and export all affected order/customer records |
| Cosmetic handling | Clean, dry storage; no extreme heat, moisture, or direct sunlight |
| Kitting | Ability to assemble Day, Night, and Complete Ritual sets using approved packout instructions |
| Shopify integration | Automatic order/inventory sync, tracking updates, cancellation handling, and split-shipment control |
| Premium packing | Branded inserts, tissue, cartons, pump protection, and photo documentation for damage claims |
| Insurance | Written inventory-loss, carrier-damage, shrinkage, and claims process |
| SLA | Same-day cutoff time, dispatch standard, inventory accuracy, and escalation response time |
| Exit terms | Month-to-month or short initial term; clear inventory-return cost if you change 3PLs |

True lot tracking means the warehouse captures lot numbers at receiving, separates lots in storage, uses FEFO logic, and associates the specific lot with each outbound order. That is essential for cosmetics traceability, complaints, and recalls.[^46_1][^46_2][^46_3]

## Documents to prepare now

Before a 3PL can quote accurately, prepare one short **CHIAREL Fulfillment Pack**:

1. **SKU master**
    - Product name, SKU, barcode, dimensions, weight, case pack, pallet configuration.
    - Shelf life, expiry format, batch-code location.
    - Bottle/jar material and fragility notes.
    - Pump lock/cap instructions.
2. **Packout guide**
    - One-product order.
    - Two-product order.
    - Three-product night ritual.
    - Four-product complete ritual.
    - Required tissue, insert, card, and shipper per order type.
3. **Returns and quarantine SOP**
    - Unopened and saleable.
    - Opened but undamaged.
    - Damaged.
    - Leaking.
    - Expired.
    - Customer irritation/adverse-event report.
    - Recall hold.
4. **Inbound SOP**
    - Italy lab releases only approved finished goods.
    - Each carton/pallet must show SKU, lot, expiry, quantity, country of origin, and carton number.
    - Lab sends an ASN before pickup.
    - 3PL counts and photographs discrepancies before receiving goods into saleable stock.
5. **Service-level agreement**
    - Order cut-off.
    - Dispatch expectation.
    - Inventory accuracy.
    - Receiving timing.
    - Returns processing timing.
    - Damage-photo standard.
    - Lot-trace report turnaround.
    - Recall/quarantine response time.

## Tax and entity checklist

For **U.S. + Italy in Wave 1**, confirm these before selling:


| Area | Action |
| :-- | :-- |
| U.S. importer | Decide which legal entity is importer of record for bulk goods from Italy |
| Customs | Engage a U.S. customs broker before first shipment; confirm HS classification, duty rate, country of origin, FDA entry requirements, and customs bond |
| U.S. sales tax | Configure Shopify tax collection based on nexus, state registrations, and advisor guidance |
| Italy VAT | Confirm Italian VAT position for domestic Italian sales |
| EU cross-border VAT | Register for OSS before France, Germany, Netherlands, and Spain go live |
| EU compliance | Responsible Person, CPSR, PIF, CPNP, labels, claims evidence, and batch records must be ready |
| Shopify | Configure separate U.S. and EU markets, currencies, prices, shipping profiles, taxes, and inventory locations |
| Accounting | Use an ecommerce accountant that can reconcile Shopify payouts, 3PL inventory, VAT/OSS, U.S. sales tax, freight, duty, and returns |

OSS is appropriate for eligible cross-border EU B2C distance sales from one EU stock location; if you later store inventory in Germany, France, or another EU country, local VAT registration can become necessary.[^46_4][^46_5][^46_6]

## Protect launch cash

Do not buy maximum packaging, sets, or inventory before proof of demand.

### Recommended initial order strategy

| Item | Recommendation |
| :-- | :-- |
| N1 | Highest inventory allocation; it is the hero |
| Essence | Medium inventory allocation |
| Terra | Medium inventory allocation |
| Recovery | Lower initial allocation unless Founding 100 remains active |
| Rigid set boxes | Small run only for PR, Founding, and complete-ritual orders |
| Folding cartons | Larger run than rigid boxes; use for individual orders |
| Refill pods | Delay high-volume refill inventory until repeat behavior is proven |
| U.S. inventory | 60–90 days of demand maximum, not six months |
| EU inventory | Hold primary reserve in Italy; replenish the 3PL based on weekly sell-through |
| Air freight | First small U.S. shipment only; use sea freight for validated replenishment |

## Final operating model

```text
ITALY
Lab/manufacturer
→ quality release
→ ASN + batch/expiry documentation
→ carrier pickup
→ Italy/EU 3PL
→ Shopify orders to Italy, France, Germany, Netherlands, Spain

ITALY
Lab/manufacturer
→ consolidated finished-goods freight
→ U.S. customs broker/importer of record
→ U.S. 3PL
→ Shopify orders to United States
```

Do not allow the U.S. 3PL to receive unapproved, un-batched, or unlabeled inventory. Do not allow either 3PL to rework, relabel, refill, or restock opened cosmetics without CHIAREL’s written QA approval. Proper returns should enter quarantine first and be inspected before any disposition decision.[^46_7][^46_8]

## Next action list

1. Send the quote emails to Bigblue, Hive, byrd, ShipBob, and ShipMonk.
2. Prepare the one-page CHIAREL Fulfillment Pack.
3. Hire or identify a U.S. customs broker.
4. Confirm Italian/EU Responsible Person, CPSR, PIF, CPNP, and VAT/OSS readiness.
5. Get 3PL quotes in the same format.
6. Compare **landed cost per shipped order**, not only storage or pick/pack fees.
7. Select one Italy/EU node and one U.S. node.
8. Run a test inbound shipment and 10–20 test orders before public launch.
9. Launch only U.S. and Italy first.
10. Add France, Germany, Netherlands, and Spain after 60–90 days of stable operations.

# is the brad more prestigious of we say Italian manufatured or US and other market or just say Dallas, USA? simple answer

Say **“Formulated and crafted in Isola del Liri, Italy”**—not “Dallas, USA” as the main product-origin message.

Italy gives CHIAREL more immediate luxury credibility because it connects the brand to craftsmanship, formulation, sensory excellence, and the Italian provenance already central to your story. Dallas should be presented as the **global headquarters / founding office**, not the product’s luxury proof point. Italy’s premium beauty ecosystem is strongly associated with formulation, sensory quality, craft positioning, and export-grade cosmetics.[^48_1][^48_2]

Use this hierarchy:

```text
Front of pack / product page:
Formulated and crafted in Isola del Liri, Italy

Brand / House page:
CHIAREL™
Founded in Dallas. Formulated in Italy.

Corporate/footer/legal:
CHIAREL™
Global Office: Dallas, Texas, USA
Formulation & Product Development: Isola del Liri, Italy
```

Do not say **“Made in Italy”** unless the final legal manufacturing origin rules support it: products must actually be sufficiently manufactured/filled there, and the claim should be confirmed with your Italian manufacturer and regulatory adviser.

This gives you the best of both:

- **Italy** = luxury, craftsmanship, sensorial formulation, product authority.
- **Dallas** = founder story, commercial leadership, American ambition.
- **CHIAREL** = a global luxury house, not an Italian-only or U.S.-only brand.

# to ready the packaging for shipment what informations will I need say on th ejar, packaging, liek formula, disclaimer, batch number etc

also can you draft: Prepare the one-page CHIAREL Fulfillment Pack.

You need two things prepared before shipment: **market-compliant consumer packaging** and a **3PL fulfillment pack**. Do not print final jars/cartons until your Italian regulatory lead reviews the final artwork for the U.S. and EU versions.

For the U.S., the label must identify the product, state net contents, list ingredients, and show the marketer/manufacturer/distributor name and address; necessary warnings also apply. For the EU, Article 19 requires the Responsible Person details, nominal content, durability/PAO, precautions, batch reference, function where needed, and INCI ingredients; the PIF/CPSR/CPNP sit behind the label.[^49_1][^49_2][^49_3]

# 1. Packaging information checklist

## On the product jar/bottle

| Required / recommended field | U.S. | EU | Notes |
| :-- | --: | --: | :-- |
| CHIAREL logo / wordmark | Yes | Yes | Libre Bodoni approved artwork |
| Product name | Yes | Yes | Example: N1 Neck \& Décolleté Renewal Emulsion |
| Product function | Yes | Yes | Example: “Neck \& Décolleté Moisturizing Emulsion” |
| Net contents | Yes | Yes | U.S.: both metric and U.S. customary units; EU: metric |
| Batch / lot code | Strongly required | Required | Must link to batch records and 3PL lot tracking |
| PAO symbol | Recommended | Required if shelf life >30 months | Example: `12M` or `6M`, based on stability data |
| Best-before date | If used | Required if shelf life is under 30 months | Must be supported by stability program |
| INCI ingredients | Yes | Yes | Descending order, finalized formula only |
| Directions | Recommended / needed for safe use | Required where needed | Must be consistent with claims and test protocol |
| Warnings / precautions | Where applicable | Required where applicable | Do not invent generic warnings; regulatory review needed |
| Responsible party details | Yes | Yes | Different information may be needed by market |
| Country of origin | Recommended | Required if imported into EU | Confirm legal origin claim with counsel/lab |
| Customer contact / adverse event contact | Required under MoCRA labeling framework | Recommended | U.S. domestic contact information must be confirmed |
| Barcode | Commercially essential | Commercially essential | EAN/GTIN for EU; UPC/GTIN for U.S. |
| Recycling/material symbols | Recommended | Recommended / increasingly important | Confirm country-specific rules |
| SKU / internal product code | Recommended | Recommended | Can be on base, carton, or label |
| QR code | Optional | Optional | Link to usage, authenticity, recycling, or ritual guide—not required labeling |

### Recommended split

**Front of vessel**

```text
CHIAREL
N1
Neck & Décolleté Renewal Emulsion
50 mL / 1.7 fl. oz.
```

**Back or base of vessel**

```text
Directions
Warnings/precautions
Batch / lot code
PAO symbol or best-before
Responsible party details
U.S. contact, if using U.S. label
EU Responsible Person, if using EU label
```

**Outer carton**

```text
Full INCI list
Full regulatory information
Barcode
Product function
Country-of-origin statement if legally verified
Storage conditions
Recycling information
Localized language panels
```


## Do not print these yet

Do not place these on final packaging until confirmed:

- “Made in Italy.”
- “Clinically proven.”
- “Dermatologist tested.”
- “Hypoallergenic.”
- “Non-toxic.”
- “Clean.”
- “Medical-grade.”
- “Repairs skin.”
- “Cellular repair.”
- “Collagen rebuilding.”
- “DNA repair.”
- “Wound healing.”
- “Fragrance-free,” until the final approved formula contains no added fragrance and the regulatory team signs off.
- Any ingredient percentage unless the final batch formula and evidence support it.

Use this only after validation:

```text
Formulated and crafted in Isola del Liri, Italy
```

Do not use “Made in Italy” unless the legal country-of-origin analysis supports it.

## Market-specific label versions

Do not force one carton to serve all future markets.


| Market | Recommended packaging version |
| :-- | :-- |
| U.S. | U.S. carton/artwork with U.S. net contents, responsible-party details, domestic contact, ingredient list, warnings |
| Italy/EU | EU carton/artwork with EU Responsible Person, metric content, PAO/best-before, required local-language precautions, INCI |
| France | EU carton plus French-required consumer information |
| Germany | EU carton plus German-required consumer information |
| Netherlands | EU carton plus Dutch-required consumer information where applicable |
| Spain | EU carton plus Spanish-required consumer information |
| UAE/GCC later | Separate Arabic/English artwork and local registration/label requirements |
| Canada later | English/French artwork |
| Latin America later | Country-specific artwork and local importer information |

The EU label needs the Responsible Person address, nominal content, durability/PAO, precautions, batch number, product function if not obvious, and INCI ingredients.[^49_4][^49_5]

# 2. Batch and 3PL labeling

Every shipping carton and pallet leaving the Italy lab must include:


| Field | Example |
| :-- | :-- |
| Brand | CHIAREL |
| Product name | N1 Neck \& Décolleté Renewal Emulsion |
| Internal SKU | N1-R-50 |
| GTIN / barcode | Final assigned GTIN |
| Batch / lot | N1-IT-2026-001 |
| Manufacture date | YYYY-MM-DD |
| Expiry / PAO | YYYY-MM-DD or 12M |
| Quantity per carton | 24 units |
| Carton number | Carton 1 of 30 |
| Gross/net weight | Actual measured weights |
| Country of origin | Only after legal confirmation |
| Storage condition | Example: Store in a cool, dry place; protect from heat/light |
| Handling mark | Fragile / do not crush / this side up, if required |
| Destination 3PL | Warehouse name, address, inbound reference |
| ASN number | Advanced Shipping Notice reference |

The 3PL must scan or record SKU, lot, expiry, carton quantity, and damage condition at receiving. It should not move stock to saleable inventory until the inbound quantity and condition reconcile with the ASN.

# 3. One-page CHIAREL Fulfillment Pack

Copy this into a one-page document for 3PLs. Replace bracketed fields before sending.

______________________________________________________________________

# CHIAREL™ Fulfillment Pack — Phase 1

**Brand:** CHIAREL™\
**Business office:** Dallas, Texas, USA\
**Formulation and finished-goods release:** Isola del Liri, Italy\
**Sales platform:** Shopify DTC\
**Phase 1 Markets:** United States, Italy; France, Germany, Netherlands, and Spain after Wave 1 validation\
**Products:** Premium fragrance-free skincare\
**Storage:** Clean, dry, ambient cosmetic storage; protect from direct sunlight, excessive heat, freezing, and moisture

## Launch SKU Master

| SKU | Product | Size | Vessel | Carton | Color | Primary market |
| :-- | :-- | --: | :-- | :-- | :-- | :-- |
| 1A-R-30 | CHIAREL Essence™ | 30 mL | Airless serum bottle | Folding carton | Peach Dust | U.S. / EU |
| 2A-R-50 | Terra Radiance Crème™ | 50 mL | Jar or airless cream vessel | Folding carton | Red Ochre | U.S. / EU |
| 4A-R-50 | Recovery Masque™ | 50 mL | Jar or airless cream vessel | Folding carton | Formal Garden | U.S. / EU |
| N1-R-50 | N1 Neck \& Décolleté Renewal Emulsion™ | 50 mL | Airless pump | Folding carton | Formal Garden | U.S. / EU |

**Important:** Final dimensions, weights, GTINs, case packs, lot format, expiry rules, and approved product images will be supplied before first inbound shipment.

## Order Types

| Order type | Typical contents | Packout requirement |
| :-- | :-- | :-- |
| Single item | One product | Product carton + protective shipper + approved insert |
| Day Ritual | Essence + Terra | Two-product approved set/carton + Day Ritual card |
| Night Ritual | Essence + Recovery + N1 | Three-product approved set/carton + Night Ritual card |
| Complete Ritual | All four products | Premium four-product set + AM/PM ritual card |
| Replenishment | One or more refill/standard products | Lightweight approved refill packaging only |

## Fulfillment Requirements

- Shopify integration with automatic order, cancellation, inventory, and tracking synchronization.
- Lot/batch tracking from inbound receipt through each customer shipment.
- Expiry-date tracking and **FEFO** picking.
- SKU, lot, quantity, expiry, and condition check at receiving.
- No saleable inventory release until inbound quantity matches CHIAREL ASN and visible damage is documented.
- Same-day or next-business-day dispatch for orders received before agreed cut-off.
- Branded insert and ritual-card insertion according to approved packout guide.
- No substitutions, relabeling, rework, repacking, or disposal without written CHIAREL authorization.
- Premium handling for glass, airless pumps, luxury cartons, and set boxes.
- Damage photos required for inbound discrepancies, outbound damage claims, and customer returns.


## Returns and Quarantine

All returns must enter **quarantine** first.


| Return status | Required action |
| :-- | :-- |
| Unopened / intact | Photograph and await CHIAREL disposition |
| Opened | Do not restock; quarantine |
| Damaged / leaking | Photograph, quarantine, report within 1 business day |
| Expired / near expiry | Quarantine; do not ship |
| Suspected adverse event | Preserve product/lot information; notify CHIAREL immediately |
| Recall hold | Stop shipment immediately; quarantine designated lot; provide affected-order report |

## Required Service Levels

| Activity | Target |
| :-- | :-- |
| Inbound receiving | Within [^49_6] business days of appointment |
| Order dispatch | Same or next business day |
| Inventory accuracy | ≥99.5% |
| Returns processing | Within [^49_7] business days |
| Damage reporting | Within [^49_8] business day |
| Lot recall report | Within [^49_9] hours of request |
| Cycle count | Monthly or upon CHIAREL request |

## Required 3PL Reports

- Daily orders shipped and tracking status.
- Weekly inventory by SKU, lot, and expiry.
- Weekly aged inventory and near-expiry report.
- Monthly storage, fulfillment, return, and damage report.
- Return/quarantine report.
- Lot-level shipment export upon request.
- Carrier performance and delivery-exception report.


## Contacts

| Function | Contact |
| :-- | :-- |
| CHIAREL Operations | [Name / email / phone] |
| CHIAREL Quality / Regulatory | [Name / email / phone] |
| Italian Lab / Release Site | [Name / email / phone] |
| Freight Forwarder | [Name / email / phone] |
| Customs Broker | [Name / email / phone] |
| 3PL Account Manager | [Name / email / phone] |

**No product may be destroyed, returned to saleable stock, relabeled, reworked, or substituted without written CHIAREL authorization.**

______________________________________________________________________

## Final preparation list

Before you send inventory to any 3PL, you need:

- Approved final formula and INCI list.
- Approved U.S. and EU artwork files.
- Approved batch-code format.
- Shelf-life and PAO decision.
- Product dimensions and weights.
- GTIN/UPC/EAN codes.
- Case pack and pallet configuration.
- Product and carton photos.
- Inbound ASN template.
- Finished-product CoA / release document.
- Stability summary.
- Cosmetic safety file references.
- Returns/quarantine SOP.
- Recall SOP.
- 3PL quality agreement.
- Cargo insurance and inventory insurance decision.
- Freight-forwarder and customs-broker contacts.
